# Divergence inventory vs upstream Ghostty

Generated 2026-11-23 by `scripts/divergence-inventory.ps1` (T516). Regenerate with:

```
powershell -NoProfile -File scripts\divergence-inventory.ps1
```

- **Fork point:** `063ac3ecc` (2026-05-07) - Update VOUCHED list (#12613)
- **Ours:** `4444cd9e7` (HEAD) - 6557 commits, 6948 files changed since the fork point
- **Upstream:** `df28358dd` (upstream/main (https://github.com/ghostty-org/ghostty.git)) - 2787 commits, 1268 files changed
- **Changed on both sides (merge risk set): 202 files** - listed in full below
- **Changed only here:** 6746 files (no upstream conflict possible)
- **Changed only upstream:** 1066 files (arrive clean on a merge)
- **minimum_zig_version:** ours `0.15.2`, upstream `0.17.1`

Re-run monthly from the daily triage (go.md step 0.6, T960) so the growing
gap is measured rather than assumed; `-Check` says whether one is due.

Status letters: M modified, A added, D deleted, R renamed, C copied.
A file we deleted that upstream kept editing (D vs M) is still merge
risk - the risk set is any path BOTH sides touched, whatever the touch.
Commit counts are non-merge commits whose diff touches the path
(equivalent to `git log --full-history --no-merges -- <path>` over the
same range), so a merge that carried a change in is not double-counted.
